## Data gaps

- **F2 Commodities.** No front-versus-next contract files are in the price cache. One commodity-list request returned generic symbols, not a dated front and next history. Carry was not run and was not replaced with the continuous contracts.
- **F3 Japan.** No dated Nikkei membership file. The current Nikkei list was not used.
- **F3 India.** No dated India membership file and no India earnings cache. India long-only was not run.
- **F3 UK.** FTSE membership is dated, but there is no FMP report-date file for the London symbols. US ADR dates were not used.
- **F3 France.** CAC membership is dated, but there is no FMP report-date file for the Euronext symbols.
- **F5 Japan.** No dated Nikkei membership file. Statements were not applied to a current constituent list.
- **F5 Europe.** Germany has statement files and no dated membership file, so a pooled Europe book was not run. France is scored on its own.
- **F5 Hard-to-borrow.** No hard-to-borrow flag is in the cache. Every short pays 1% per year. The 5% branch was not used.
- **F6 Hong Kong.** No dated Hang Seng membership file and no Hong Kong price cache in the strategy prices. The long-only book was not run.

## Early exits

A name that stopped trading during a hold was closed at its last close and kept. A name with no bar on the entry date was not counted.

F3 0. F4 0. F5 0. F6 0.

Cached earnings rows 77315, rows with a before-open or after-close field 0. stable/earnings AAPL rows 165; time fields: none; rows with a time value: 0. Entry stays at the open of d0+2.

## What was not substituted

Japan, India, Hong Kong, and Germany have no dated membership file in this study, so those markets were not filled with a current constituent list. Commodity carry was not filled with the continuous micro contracts. A market return was not used as an industry return. Industry and sector labels used by F4 and F6 are current FMP profile fields, marked CURRENT_INDUSTRY_LABELS. They are not point-in-time. GBPUSD and EURUSD caches begin in 2016, so Altman Z before the first FX bar is missing and is not filled with a later rate.
